## 82. Campaign with us | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_campaign-with-us.html*

# Campaign with us

[No child should have to pay the price of poverty](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/child-poverty/)

Join us, take a stand, and raise your voice for better and brighter childhoods

On this page:
[Why we campaign](#why-we-campaign)
|
[Where we campaign](#we-campaign-in-three-areas)
|
[Our latest campaigns](#our-latest-campaigns)
|
[Making childhood a national priority](#making-childhood-a-national-priority-the-childrens-charities-coalition)
|
[The difference we've made](#the-difference-weve-made)
|
[Become an Action for Children Campaigner](#become-an-action-for-children-campaigner)
.

## Why we campaign

Right now, we’re seeing a system that continues to fail children. Our frontline services see more children at risk of harm, rising numbers of children taken into care, and an increase in child poverty. This isn’t just bad luck.

**We won’t stop fighting until every child grows up safe and happy**
.

But alone, our voice isn’t enough. Change is only possible with supporters like you behind us.

Sign a petition, contact your MP, or share our campaigns with friends and family. When you do, you’re joining a movement improving the lives of children in the UK

**Help us make change happen**

Hear about our latest campaigns

[Sign up
(opens in a new tab)](https://take.actionforchildren.org.uk/page/88416/subscribe/1?ea.tracking.id=afc_website_cp)

## We campaign in four areas

Choose a campaign and join us today. In just a few minutes, you can help us make a difference.

You can also stay up-to-date by reading
[our latest blogs.](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/blog/?news_type=1&news_topic=)

**Child poverty**

The government can't be blind to the 4 million children living in poverty. We're calling on them to act now and give every child a fair start in life.

[Take action now](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/child-poverty/)

**Children's social care**

Families facing problems and children in care deserve quality support to help them thrive. We’re calling for a system reset.

[Join the fight](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/childrens-social-care/)

**Criminal exploitation of children**

Going missing. Told to carry a knife. Terrified. Children are being trapped in exploitation. They need protections to keep them safe.

[Stand with us](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/our-work-and-impact/policy-work-campaigns-and-research/criminally-exploited-children/)

**Protecting Universal Credit for disabled young people**

Learn how we’re supporting young people facing barriers to work, including access to social security.

[Read our briefing](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/universal-credit-disabled-young-people/)

## Our latest campaigns

**Paying the Price**

We're calling on the government to make sure no child has to pay the price of poverty.

[It's time to act](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/child-poverty/)

**A Place to Call Home**

We're campaigning for lasting change in the children’s social care system.

[Join us](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/childrens-social-care/a-place-to-call-home/)

> "Bad things happened in my life before I went into care. I’m still recovering from them. But I’m also still recovering from my time in care. Does that seem right?"
>
> Alex\*, young campaigner

### Making childhood a national priority: The Children's Charities Coalition

**The UK could be the best place in the world to grow up. But right now, it isn’t.**

That's why we've been working closely with Barnardo’s, The Children’s Society, the National Children’s Bureau and NSPCC. Together, we're calling for safer, happier, healthier childhoods to be a national priority.

**The Children's Charities Coalition**

We’re working with leading children’s charities to ensure children’s voices are heard.

[Find out more](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/the-childrens-charities-coalition/)

## The difference we've made

Our campaigners have acted to change lives. More children are now protected because of these changes to laws and policies.

**Here’s what we’ve achieved with your support:**

* UK law now
  [recognises children](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/blog/domestic-abuse-bill-community-based-support/)
  affected by domestic abuse as victims in their own right.
* In England and Wales,
  [sustained emotional abuse](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/neglect-law/)
  against children is now a crime.
* In Wales, young people can
  [choose to stay in foster care](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/care-law-in-wales/)
  for longer.

## Become an Action for Children campaigner

Sign up to be the first to know about our latest campaigns and how you can support us. Our thousands of campaigners are making a difference – but there’s more to do. Join them.

**Help us make change happen**

Hear about our latest campaigns

[Sign up
(opens in a new tab)](https://take.actionforchildren.org.uk/page/88416/subscribe/1?ea.tracking.id=afc_website_cp)

## More about Action for Children

### What we do

We protect and support children and young people. We make sure their voices are heard. And we campaign to bring lasting improvements to their lives.

Today, we run 342 services in local communities across the UK, in schools and online.

In our nurseries and children's centres, staff make sure children get the best start in life. Our foster and modern residential services find children and young people safe and loving homes.

We step in to protect children from neglect and abuse and help those who care for others. We're also here for parents, through projects like Parent Talk, our online support service.

### Our impact

We’ve been helping children for more than 150 years. In 2024/25, we helped 551,400 children, young people and families. This was only possible with your support.

* We helped more than 10,740 disabled children, young people and families in 2024/25.
* We've supported 484,650 parents in 2024/25 through our family support services.

## Share this page

[Share on Twitter](https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=Add%20your%20voice%20to%20our%20campaigns%20and%20petitions%20for%20better%20and%20brighter%20childhoods.%20Here%27s%20how%20to%20get%20involved.&url=https%3A//www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/&via=actnforchildren "Share on Twitter")

[Share on Facebook](https://www.facebook.com/dialog/feed?app_id=2550348755273253&link=https%3A//www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/&name=Campaign%20with%20us&description=Add%20your%20voice%20to%20our%20campaigns%20and%20petitions%20for%20better%20and%20brighter%20childhoods.%20Here%27s%20how%20to%20get%20involved.&redirect_uri=https%3A//www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/campaign-with-us/ "Share on Facebook")

## 83. Charity runs | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_fundraise-with-us_join-a-challenge-event_charity-runs.html*

# Charity runs

Join Team Action for Children

We have places in some of the UK's best races. Join us today and help support vulnerable children, young people, and families across the UK with a charity run. We would love to welcome you to the team.

## Sign up for a charity run

### Royal Parks Half Marathon 2026

Join Team Action for Children and run one of Britain’s best half marathon courses.

## 84. Bedford Running Festival | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_fundraise-with-us_join-a-challenge-event_charity-runs_bedford-running-festival.html*

# Bedford Running Festival 2026

Russell Park, Bedford





Sep 5
—
Sep 6

[Register now

(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/fundraise-with-us/join-a-challenge-event/charity-runs/bedford-running-festival/sign-up-for-the-bedford-running-festival/)

Join us for the Bedford Running Festival 2026!

Get ready for an incredible weekend of running, celebration, and community spirit at the
**Bedford Running Festival**
!

Taking place on the first weekend of September, this event is perfect for everyone, from first-time runners and families to club athletes and charity fundraising groups.

With a range of distances to choose from, including the
**Mini Miles**
**Fun Run**
(where children under 8 must be accompanied by an adult with their own ticket), all can participate.

Take on the
**5K**
, the atmospheric
**Twilight 10K**
, a
**Half Marathon**
, or a
**20-Miler**
- there’s something for all abilities.

## Registration fees

Registration costs for the Bedford Running Festival vary from £17-39, depending on which event you want to take part in:

* **Mini miles (family fun run)**
  : £12
* **5K**
  : £19-£21
* **Twilight 10K**
  : £26-£28
* **Bedford Half Marathon**
  : £35-£37
* **20 miles**
  : £37-£39

## What you'll get

* Action for Children branded running vest to wear on the day of your event for everyone who raises £100 or more
* Access to exclusive online fundraising and training hub with training tips from our professional running coach
* Support from our dedicated events team to help you smash your fundraising target

## Register now

**Sign up for the Bedford Running Festival**

[Register now
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/fundraise-with-us/join-a-challenge-event/charity-runs/bedford-running-festival/sign-up-for-the-bedford-running-festival/)

Taking part in this challenge is not only an incredible personal achievement but you’ll also be helping us to give vulnerable children a safe and happy childhood. Sign up and start your fundraising now by
[setting up a Just Giving page](https://www.justgiving.com/charity/actionforchildren)
.

## Event details

### Event dates

Sep 05, 2026
- Sep 06, 2026

### Address

* Russell Park
* The Embankment
* Bedford
* MK40 3RH

### Email

[challenge@actionforchildren.org.uk](mailto:challenge@actionforchildren.org.uk)

## 85. Cardiff Half Marathon 2026 | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_fundraise-with-us_join-a-challenge-event_charity-runs_cardiff-half-marathon.html*

# Cardiff Half Marathon 2026

Cardiff





4th Oct

[Get involved](#sign-up-information)

Take part in Wales’ largest mass participation event by running the Cardiff Half Marathon.

The flat, fast-paced Cardiff Half Marathon route passes the city’s most breath-taking scenery and iconic landmarks. Along the way, enjoy sights like Cardiff Castle, Principality Stadium, Civic Centre, and the stunning Cardiff Bay.

> I absolutely love the Cardiff Half Marathon route. It's definitely one of the most scenic that I've run. I have 10+ half marathons so far and this year it was the best supported one. Thank to you all at Action for Children for all of the support. I will be back again next year!
>
> H Jones, Action for Children runner

**Take on the challenge**

And help us reach more vulnerable children across the UK

[Sign up](#sign-up-information)

**Have your own place?**

You can use your own place to fundraise for Action for Children

[Join us](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/fundraise-with-us/join-a-challenge-event/charity-runs/cardiff-half-marathon/use-your-own-place-at-the-cardiff-half-marathon-2026/)

## Why take part?

As well as taking part in an incredible event, you’ll be helping more children to have safe and happy childhoods and the foundations they need to thrive. We ask for a
**£30 registration donation**
to secure your place and a
**minimum fundraising target of £350.**

In return, you’ll get:

* Free Action for Children branded running vest to wear on the day of your event.
* Tailored training guides and tips from our professional running coach.
* Dedicated support from our fundraising experts.
* Digital support pack with fundraising ideas and materials.
* The opportunity to join our Strava and Facebook groups.

## Sign up information

**Registration fee: £30
  
Minimum fundraising target: £350**

Complete the form below to register your interest in the Cardiff Half Marathon with Action for Children. We'll email you with details on how to get signed up and welcome you to the team!

If you have any questions about the Cardiff Half Marathon or how to sign up and start fundraising, email
[challenge@actionforchildren.org.uk](mailto:challenge@actionforchildren.org.uk)
.

## Event details

### Event date

Oct 04, 2026

### Phone

0300 123 2112

### Email

[challenge@actionforchildren.org.uk](mailto:challenge@actionforchildren.org.uk)

## 86. Great North Run 2026 | Run for charity | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_fundraise-with-us_join-a-challenge-event_charity-runs_great-north-run-2026.html*

# Great North Run 2026

Newcastle





13th Sep

[Sign up now!](#sport-event-application-form)

Join team Action for Children

**REGISTRATION FOR THIS EVENT IS NOW CLOSED!**
  
  
On Sunday 13th September 2026 the world’s biggest half marathon returns to the North East. Great North Run charity places are available with Action for Children.

By running for us, you'll not only get
[amazing benefits](#why-join-our-team)
, you'll also be supporting vulnerable children in the UK.

### Apply for a charity place to secure your place at Great North Run 2026

**Entry donation: £40**

**Minimum fundraising target: £400**

**Apply for a charity place**

Join Team Action for Children at the Great North Run

[Sign up](#register-your-interest-in-an-action-for-children-charity-place)

**Do you have a ballot place?**

You can use your ballot place to run for Action for Children

[Sign up now](#successful-in-the-ballot-run-for-our-charity)

## Why join our team

Whether you have a
**ballot place**
or a
**charity place**
with us, all Action for Children runners will receive:

* **Hero's welcome, refreshments and photos**
  with your medal. You'll find us in our post-race marquee.
* **Running vest:**
  Free Action for Children vest with iron-on letters so everyone can cheer your name.
* **Tailored training guides**
  and tips from our professional running coach Colin Thomas.
* **Fundraising support:**
  Guidance from your local fundraiser all the way up to event day and beyond, so you can smash your fundraising target.
* **And lots of cheers!**
  Be cheered on by our amazing and loud cheer squad to keep you going to the finish line.

**Apply for a charity place**

Join Team Action for Children at the Great North Run

[Sign up](#register-your-interest-in-an-action-for-children-charity-place)

Item 1 of 7

If you get a place in the ballot, you can support Action for Children and receive all the same fantastic benefits as runners with our charity places. We would love to have you! Simply complete the form below to join our team and we'll be in touch to help you in your fundraising journey!
  
***Please note this form is only for those who have entered the ballot.***

and

across the UK. And this year's is going to be bigger, bouncier, and better than ever before. You'll be jumping and laughing your way to the finish line.

11th Oct

[Sign up](#sport-event-application-form)

Join Team Action for Children and run one of Britain’s best half marathon courses.

Running through four of London’s iconic Royal Parks – Hyde Park, Green Park, St James’ Park, and Kensington Gardens – this is a
**fast and flat Half Marathon course**
.

Whether you’re a seasoned runner or taking on your first half, The Royal Parks Half Marathon is the
**perfect event**
for you.

Taking part in this challenge is not only an incredible personal achievement, but you’ll also be helping us to give
**vulnerable children**
a safe and happy childhood. We’ll support you every step of the way.

**Take on the challenge**

**Royal Parks Half**

Our charity places are limited and costly, so we ask all runners to help us maximise their impact. By accepting a place, you agree to:

## 90. Donate in memory of someone | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_make-a-donation_donate-in-memory-of-someone.html*

# Donate in memory of someone

Giving in memory of a loved one can help us support safe and happy lives for vulnerable children

Celebrate the life of a loved one by transforming a child’s life in their memory.

Your donations could help us deliver life-changing support for thousands of children and families locked in poverty.

## How to donate

Create a tribute to someone special by donating or fundraising in memory through
[JustGiving](https://www.justgiving.com/for-fundraising/in-memory)
or
[MuchLoved](https://www.muchloved.com/)
.

You can also make a one-off donation in memory of a loved one.

[Donate in memory](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/donate-now/)

## We're here to help

If you would like more information about donating in memory of a loved one, please don’t hesitate to get in touch. We’re always happy to help.

Where possible please contact us via email or phone (Monday to Friday, 9am - 5pm). Especially if you need an urgent response.

**Call us**

[0300 123 2112](tel:0300 123 2112)

**Email us**

[inmemory@actionforchildren.org.uk](mailto:inmemory@actionforchildren.org.uk)

**Write to us**
  
We currently have reduced access to our post so it may take longer to reply than usual. However, rest assured we will be in touch as soon as possible.

Our address is: Legacy Team, Action for Children, 3 The Boulevard, Ascot Road, Watford WD18 8AG

## 91. Leave a gift in your will | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_make-a-donation_leave-a-gift-in-your-will.html*

# Leave a gift in your will

Gifts in wills help make sure support is there when it’s needed.

Whether you’re looking for information about gifts in wills or are a first-time executor, we’re here to help.

**How to leave a gift in your will**

We’re here to guide you.

[Find out more
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/how-to-leave-a-gift-in-your-will/)

**Free gift in wills info pack**

Start your journey today by requesting a free guide to leaving a gift in your will.

[Order your pack
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/further-information/)

**Sky of Stars**

Find your loved one in our Sky of Stars

[Visit our Tribute site
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/sky-of-stars/)

**Frequently asked questions**

Have a question? We're here to help.

[Read our FAQs
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/gifts-in-wills-faqs/)

**Help for executors and solicitors**

We know your job is tough, we're here to help.

[Read more
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/help-for-executors-and-solicitors/)

**Our legacy promise**

This is our promise to you.

[Find out more
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/our-legacy-promise/)

**Can't find what you're looking for?**

[Contact us here](#how-to-contact-us)

## Why leave a gift in your will?

### You could keep a child safe. Make sure they’re healthy and happy. And help them to achieve incredible things.

A gift in your will could help transform a child’s life. Leaving even a small percentage (
[a residuary legacy](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/how-to-leave-a-gift-in-your-will/)
) will go a long way. These gifts are inflation proof and exempt from inheritance tax.

For vulnerable children, we’re family. And you can be too. Now, and for generations to come.

Gifts in wills contribute to changing the lives of 551,400 children, young people and their families.

**There’s still so much more work to be done.**

[Watch "Why would you leave a gift in your will for Action for Children?" on YouTube](https://youtu.be/A6gp7Fa2ojw?feature=shared)

> Action for Children have offered us so much support and always do their best to help. They truly want to help look after families. Every child should be happy and healthy, and that's what they are doing with their work.
>
> Ram, whose family was supported by Action for Children

## I don't have an existing will

1. List what you own.
   [**Download our will planner**](https://media.actionforchildren.org.uk/documents/Legacy_Will_Planner_Digital_nPva3b8.pdf)
   .
2. Decide who to leave it to. We would always encourage you to look after your family and friends first and seek independent legal advice.
3. Choose your executors. That’s somebody who is going to manage your estate and carry out your wishes.
4. Write your will. As a valued supporter you can have your will written for free. You have the choice to write your will online. Or talk to a specialist by phone.
   [**Get your free will now**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/free-will-offers/)
   .
5. Complete our
   [**pledge form**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/pledger-form/)
   to let us know.

**Write your will for free**

Gifts in wills help make sure support is available for generations to come. Write yours for free online with our trusted partner.

[Get your free will offer
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/free-will-offers/)

## I already have a will

1. Complete a
   [**codicil form**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/documents/3229/ACT-0032-002_Codicil_form_A4_2pp_AW_Digital_Editable.pdf)
   . Then attach it to your will and provide a copy to your solicitor.
2. Complete our
   [**pledge form**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/pledger-form/)
   .

## How to contact us

**Phone -**
Call us Monday - Friday, 9am - 5pm on
[**0300 123 2112**](tel:03001232112)
- our Supporter Services team are happy to help.

**Email -**
[**giftsinwills@actionforchildren.org.uk**](mailto:giftsinwills@actionforchildren.org.uk?subject=Enquiry%20about%20leaving%20a%20gift%20in%20my%20will)
- to ensure a quicker response, scan and email documents to us where possible.

**Post -**
Write to: Legacy Team, Action for Children, 3 The Boulevard, Ascot Road, Watford, WD18 8AG.

**Pledger form -**
Use our
[**pledger form**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/pledger-form/)
to let us know that you have left a gift or are thinking about leaving a gift.

## 92. Get your will written for free | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_make-a-donation_leave-a-gift-in-your-will_free-will-offers.html*

# Our will-writing partner

As a valued Action for Children supporter, you can have your will written for free, with our will writing partner Farewill.

You can use this free service whether or not you include Action for Children in your will. But a gift of any size would make a difference.

You have the choice to write your will online, or a specialist can phone you.

[**Get your free will now**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/free-will-offers/claim-your-free-will-now/)
or read on for more details.

## Make a will online

You could write your will online in a few easy steps with our partner Farewill, the UK's number one online will-writing service. You can write your will in as little as 15 minutes, with live, online support from specialists.

[Find out more](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/free-will-offers/claim-your-free-will-now/)

Alternatively, if you would prefer not to use the free will service, you can find a local solicitor by visiting
[The Law Society](https://solicitors.lawsociety.org.uk/)
.

## How do I leave a gift in my will?

Whether you choose to write your will using our trusted online will provider or a private solicitor - you will be given guidance throughout the process.

When writing your will online with Farewill, you will be asked if you would like to leave a gift to charity.

You can then decide if you would like to leave a specific amount of money or a percentage of your estate. And they will take care of the rest.

We always recommend you think of friends and family first, and get independent legal advice. If you change your mind, you can remove Action for Children from your will easily at any time. You don’t have to tell us.

> I have been a supporter of Action for Children for over 50 years – I first started as a girl selling sunny smiles. I have seen first hand the effects of child abuse, poverty and disability on children and their families as a children’s nurse and realise the importance of the work of Action for Children. I am happy to leave a legacy in my will to continue helping those who need it most.
>
> Elizabeth, Action for Children Legacy Pledger

## About Farewill

### Where does the organisation cover?

Farewill’s online will-writing services cover England and Wales. There are phone options for Scotland and Northern Ireland.

Visit
<https://farewill.com/afc-phone>
for more information.

### How does it work?

Farewill guide you step-by-step through a series of questions so they can create your personalised will.

### How are the wills written?

Farewill work with expert legal advisors to make sure they use the latest in legal precedents in all their wills. Based on the information you enter on the site, they personalise your will to you and draft it with the same structure and content lawyers use.

### How long does it take?

Most people finish in 10-15 minutes, although it’s recommended you take your time to fully understand everything. If you need more time, pause and come back. Your information will still be there.

### What is covered in the Farewill free will offer?

This offer will cover the cost of having your will written and made legally binding.

There is an optional £10 per year fee to have unlimited access to update your will. You can opt into and out of this service.

Farewill are not yet able to provide wills to the following groups of people:

* People with disabled dependants.
* People who want to set up trusts mitigating their inheritance tax liability.
* People with significant estates outside of the UK.
* People who lack testamentary capacity.
* People who are blind, illiterate, or significantly visually impaired.
* People domiciled outside of England or Wales.

### How do you make your will legally binding?

For a will to be legally binding, it must be written down and signed by the person whose will it is and two witnesses. Once this is done, it is legally valid.

There are four important criteria that all valid wills must meet:

1. The person making the will must be over 18, not under any undue influence, and have "testamentary capacity".
2. The will must be properly written and formatted.
3. The will must be signed by the person making the will and two witnesses (ordinary people, not solicitors).
4. The person making the will must understand what is in it, the effect that it will have, and approve of its content.

### How should you store your will?

You can store a will in your own home, or pay for a will storage service. If you keep it at home, put it somewhere safe and make sure you tell your executors where it is.

## 94. Honouring our heritage | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_make-a-donation_leave-a-gift-in-your-will_honouring-our-heritage.html*

# Honouring our heritage

In 1869, after finding two boys sheltering at Waterloo station, Methodist minister Reverend Dr Thomas Bowman Stephenson dedicated his life to helping vulnerable children.

Fred and George, the first two boys found by Revd Dr. Stephenson

Passionate about social justice, Revd Dr Stephenson challenged the Methodist Church to act, leading to the founding of the
**National Children’s Home**
, which became
**Action for Children**
in 2008.

Much has changed since then, but our mission remains the same -
**to give every child a safe and happy childhood.**

157

years of helping vulnerable children and families

551,400

Children & families helped last year

342

Services in communities across the UK

## Leave a legacy of love, just like Elizabeth

Remembering
**Action for Children in your will**
is one of many meaningful ways to support the children, young people and families we work with.

Elizabeth has been a supporter for
**over 50 years**
, beginning as a girl selling Sunny Smiles.

In her will, she's chosen to leave a legacy of love for vulnerable children, helping ensure support is there for generations to come.

"As a children's nurse, I've seen the effects of child abuse, poverty, and disability on children and their families, and realise the
**importance**
of Action for Children's work."

> I am happy to leave a legacy in my will to continue helping those who need it the most.
>
> Elizabeth, who has pledged a legacy gift to Action for Children

Have your will written for free

[Find out more](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/legacy-free-will-offer-form-1/)

## A look through the years

Item 1 of 6

Throughout June, we're asking you to join us in remembering the kindness of Revd Dr Stephenson and his work. You can do this by following our weekly prayer guides:

<a id="actionforchildrenorguk_support-us_make-a-donation_leave-a-gift-in-your-will_how-to-leave-a-gift-in-your-will"></a>

## 95. How to leave a gift in your will | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_make-a-donation_leave-a-gift-in-your-will_how-to-leave-a-gift-in-your-will.html*

# How to leave a gift in your will

Follow these simple steps to help us continue our work with children and young people in your name

Thank you for considering leaving us a gift in your will. This page will walk you through the process while answering some of the questions you might have.

**Get your free guide**

Download a free information pack

[Free information pack](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/further-information/)

## Where do I start?

You have two options, depending on whether you already have a will or not.

### I don't have an existing will

1. List what you own.
   [Download our will planner](/documents/3721/Legacy_Will_Planner_Digital_ggGruTV.pdf)
   .
2. Decide who to leave it to. We would always encourage you to look after your family and friends first and seek independent legal advice.
3. Choose your executors. That’s somebody who is going to manage your estate and carry out your wishes.
4. Write your will. As a valued supporter you can have your will written for free. You have the choice to write your will online. Or talk to a specialist by phone or online video.
   [Get your free will now](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/free-will-offers/)
   .
5. Complete our
   [pledge form](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/pledger-form/)
   to let us know.

### I already have a will

1. Complete a
   [codicil form](/documents/3229/ACT-0032-002_Codicil_form_A4_2pp_AW_Digital_Editable.pdf)
   . Then attach it to your will and provide a copy to your solicitor.
2. Complete our
   [pledge form](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/pledger-form/)
   .

## What do I write in my will?

### Our address and charity number

If you'd like to include Action for Children in your will, please ask your solicitor or will writing service to use our full name, address and charity number to ensure we receive the gift:

Name: Action for Children
  
Address: 3 The Boulevard, Ascot Road, Watford, WD18 8AG
  
Registered charity numbers: 1097940 (England) and SC038092 (Scotland)

### Example wording for your will

**Residuary**
  
I give the residue of my estate to Action for Children, 3 The Boulevard, Ascot Road, Watford, WD18 8AG, (Registered Charity Number 1097940) for its general charitable purposes. I further direct that the receipt of the Chief Executive or other proper officer of the said charity for the time being shall be a full and sufficient discharge for the said gift.

**Pecuniary/ Specific**

I give the sum of £\_\_\_\_\_ (or the item specified) to Action for Children, 3 The Boulevard, Ascot Road, Watford, WD18 8AG, (Registered Charity Number 1097940) for its general charitable purposes. I further direct that the receipt of the Chief Executive or other proper officer of the said charity for the time being shall be a full and sufficient discharge for the said gift.

*For national gifts to benefit Scotland directly change charity number to (Registered Charity Number SC-038092)*

## What types of gifts can I leave?

### A share of your estate

After you have provided for your loved ones, you can leave a share of what remains to charity. This is called as a "residuary gift". Many people prefer to give a residuary gift because it keeps in line with inflation, so does not lose value over time.

### A cash gift

This is when you leave a fixed amount of money. This is known as a "pecuniary gift".

### A specific gift

Is a particular item, like personal possessions, land or shares.

## Will my gift to Action for Children be subject to tax?

Most gifts are exempt from tax. We usually receive the full amount.

Your gift may also bring the total value of your estate below the inheritance tax threshold. Or you may qualify to pay inheritance tax at a reduced rate if you leave a gift to charity. We recommend you talk to a solicitor for more details.

Use this calculator to work out if you qualify.

[Inheritance tax calculator
(opens in a new tab)](https://www.gov.uk/inheritance-tax-reduced-rate-calculator)

## Why is a will important?

A will is vital at all stages of your life. It can protect your loved ones, causes closest to you and ensure your memory lives on.

Of course, we know that loved ones always come first. We're asking that you remember us once your loved ones' needs are met. If you leave a gift we advise letting your loved ones know.

We also always recommend you seek independent legal advice, to ensure all legal formalities are covered and that your will is valid.

The Legacy team are always happy to help you through the process. For a complete guide, get your information pack.

[Get your free guide](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/further-information/)

## Should I tell you about my gift?

That's up to you. We’d love to hear from you! It'll give us the opportunity to thank you. And you will receive a pin badge and special updates if you wish. It also ensures we can make vital plans for the future.

Rest assured any information shared with us is confidential. And you can change your mind at any time.

[Let us know](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/pledger-form/)

## I need more help

If you would like more information please do not hesitate to get in touch. We're always happy to help.

Call:
[0300 123 2112](tel:0300 123 2112)
  
Email:
[giftsinwills@actionforchildren.org.uk](mailto:giftsinwills@actionforchildren.org.uk)

**Get your free guide**

Download a free information pack

[Free information pack](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/make-a-donation/leave-a-gift-in-your-will/further-information/)

## 97. Corporate partnerships | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_partner-with-us_corporate-partnerships.html*

# Corporate partnerships

As a UK-wide charity, we offer you the chance to change the lives of children and young people in local communities.

Last year we helped over
[**551,400 children and families**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/our-work-and-impact/our-impact/)
across the UK. Some of the services we provide include financial assistance, help with housing, mental health support and training.

We are a trusted charity partner for brands both big and small. Our amazing partnerships have helped us make an impact in so many ways.

## Our current corporate partners



[Meet our partners](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/partner-with-us/corporate-partnerships/our-incredible-corporate-partners/)

## Why partner with us?

* Your dedicated Partnership Manager will provide everything you need to make our partnership a success. You’ll also get support from our media team, senior management team and community fundraisers.
* We’ll create a calendar of events, communications and fundraising activities to engage your employees and customers.
* We’ll organise special volunteering opportunities, so employees can see the difference they’re making.
* Almost 80% of the UK’s population live less than 10 miles from an Action for Children service, so your employees and customers will be supporting vulnerable children in their local communities.

**Partner with us**

Complete this form and we'll be in touch

[Enquire now](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/partner-with-us/partner-with-us-form/)

## Don't just take our word for it...

> We have worked with many charities through our Community Investment programme and Action for Children has been particularly impressive to work with.
>
> Penny Amatt, Corporate Responsibility and Community Investment Manager, Royal Mail Group

Item 1 of 5

We can work with you to create a partnership package that delivers profound and lasting changes to children and young people.

Working with us can also help customers care more about your brand, and help your employees feel proud to work for you.

> Action for Children understands the value and importance of a true partnership. They've worked hard to quickly understand CBRE’s business, and to help our staff empathise with the challenges facing the children and young people in care. Action for Children has exceeded our expectations at every level.
>
> Steve Timbs, Executive Director, CBRE

**Partner with us**

Complete this form and we'll be in touch

[Enquire now](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/partner-with-us/partner-with-us-form/)

## 98. Volunteer with us | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_volunteer-with-us.html*

# Volunteer with us

Make a real difference to vulnerable children and families across the UK by volunteering with us.

## Why volunteer

Volunteering with Action for Children is a meaningful way to help some of the UK’s most vulnerable children and families.

Along the way, you'll gain
**valuable experience**
,
**meet new people**
, and
**learn new skills**
.

551,400

Children & families helped last year

342

Services in communities across the UK

156 years

We've been helping children since 1869

Many volunteers say the experience is good for their wellbeing and self-development. Whatever your career or life experience, we have a role for you.

**Ready to volunteer?**

Find opportunities in your area and start your volunteering journey

[Get started
(opens in a new tab)](https://applytovolunteer.actionforchildren.org.uk/)

## Support and training

All our voluntary roles are supported. We’ll give you an induction and relevant training, and you’ll always have space to raise issues.

You don’t need specific skills for many of our roles. All we ask for is enthusiasm and a willingness to get involved. We’ll support your professional development with references and news about internal vacancies. As a volunteer, you’ll also be able to access our catalogue of E-learning courses.

We are so proud to have achieved the Investing in Volunteers award. Our volunteers feel recognised, supported, and proud to donate their time and experience to help vulnerable children and young people.

Volunteers painting at one of our children's services.

## What does volunteering involve?

There isn’t just one way to be a volunteer. We have a range of roles that flex around the time you have to offer and the skills you want to use. You might work with your local playgroup, support young asylum seekers, or provide admin support.

> The best part of being a mentor is the connections you make with your matched young person. I remember the first time mine asked me for advice – it was unexpected and heartwarming.
>
> Bernice, Young Person’s Mentor, London

### Who can volunteer?

Volunteering is open to everyone, from all backgrounds. There’s no upper age limit, though you’ll need to be at least 14 to apply (16 or 18 for some roles). We’re especially keen to hear from people with disabilities, or who identify as Black, Asian or another minority ethnic group.

You may be unsure how volunteering fits your background, health or personal circumstances. But whatever your life experience, we’d value your contribution.

You can volunteer if you’re an asylum seeker. Volunteering won’t affect any benefits you get. We’ll support your application if you have a health condition.

**Discover our volunteer roles**
[Explore roles](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/volunteer-with-us/register-your-interest-volunteering/)

## Some of our current featured roles include:

### Young person's mentor (Independent Visitor)

Become an Independent Visitor to guide, listen to, and befriend a young person in care.

As an Independent Visitor, you'll be trained and matched with a young person in care, and typically meet with them every month. Your role is to give your mentee the opportunity to build a trusting relationship with a supportive adult outside the care system, to be there for them, introduce them to new activities, and spend quality time together.

[Find out more](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/how-we-can-help/our-local-services/find-our-services-near-you/london-independent-visitors-service/)

### Helping at events

Can you help us set-up events, and be there to support visitors, staff and fundraisers? Take a
[look at our events](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/fundraise-with-us/)
, then email us stating which you’d like to help at.

[Get in touch](mailto:event.volunteers@actionforchildren.org.uk)

### Out Action Squads

Join a wonderful family of community event volunteers to raise vital funds for our work. Set up your own Action Squad or join one in your local area.

[Join an Action Squad](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/volunteer-with-us/action-squads/)

[Explore our different roles and register your interest](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/volunteer-with-us/register-your-interest-volunteering/)

> Having someone independent from social services in my life as a child was so important to me. [...] She supported me and advocated for me from a young age when I didn’t even know I needed it. [...] She believed in me, and people don’t often believe in us as children in care.
>
> Louise, a care-experienced young person supported by Action for Children

[Watch "Meet Denise" on YouTube](https://youtu.be/EMTxJ2Br89c?si=B_m8onmVYV-Ku8X8)

Hear from Denise, a volunteer who has helped us create an e-learning program about inclusive communication.

**Ready to volunteer?**

Find opportunities in your area and start your volunteering journey

[Get started
(opens in a new tab)](https://applytovolunteer.actionforchildren.org.uk/)

## 99. Start your volunteer journey with us | Action For Children

*Source file: actionforchildren.org.uk_support-us_volunteer-with-us_register-your-interest-volunteering.html*

# Register your interest

We're really pleased that you're thinking of volunteering with us!

Our roles are
**flexible**
and designed to fit around your lifestyle, so you can take part when it suits you.

Below are just a few of the ways you could support children, young people and families.

You can also tell us which roles you're interested in by filling out our
[**form**](#register-your-interest)
.

### Volunteer opportunities

### Join us at a cheer point

From iconic events like the
**London Marathon**
and
**Great North Run**
to half marathons and charity challenges, our fantastic runners take part in events across the UK.

We’re always looking for volunteers to support our runners, from
**cheering them on**
along the route, to
**helping at post-race receptions**
.

Being part of a cheer squad is great fun. You'll soak up the atmosphere, show your support, and give our runners a much-needed boost when it matters most.

### Be a collection tin coordinator

This is a
**flexible**
role you can really make your own, involving placing collection tins in your local area and sending in the donations once they’re full.

Every tin collection can help make a real difference to children and young people.

### Volunteer at our high-value events

From
[**Boycott Your Bed**](https://www.actionforchildren.org.uk/support-us/boycott-your-bed/)
sleepout events to formal lunches and business quizzes, we host fundraising events throughout the year and across the UK.

We're always grateful for volunteers who can lend a hand on the day or evening.

Fill out the
[**form**](#register-your-interest)
to let us know where you’re based, and we’ll share details of upcoming events near you and how you can get involved.

### Become a Methodist Champion or Church Contact volunteer

If you're part of the
**Methodist Church**
, we'd love to hear from you!

We work closely with Methodist Churches and are always looking for volunteers to act as a
**friendly link**
between the church and the Action for Children team.

You'll help support
**communication**
,
**coordination**
and
**simple activities**
that keep the church connected to our work with children and families.

### Be an Action for Children speaker

If you enjoy speaking to groups, you could play a
**key role**
in sharing our story.

Our volunteer speakers attend events like WI and Rotary Club meetings, visit small businesses, and take part in cheque presentations to help raise awareness in local communities.

You’ll be supported
**every step of the way**
, with training and a template script provided.

### Join or set up an Action Squad

Action Squads are our
**community fundraising groups**
who plan fun and creative activities all year round.

If you already have a great idea or simply enjoy bringing people together for a good cause, this is a fantastic way to get involved locally while being part of something bigger.

### Become an Action for Children Challenge Champion

Our Challenge Champions are passionate supporters who help
**inspire**
and
**encourage**
others to take part in sporting and challenge events for Action for Children.

If you love motivating others and sharing your experience, this role could be perfect for you.

### What happens next?

Please use the
[**form**](#register-your-interest)
below to tell us a little bit about yourself - including where you're based and the roles you're interested in.

From there, we'll send you a full role description and everything you need to get started.

If the role you’re interested in happens annually, you can also tell us how you’d prefer us to stay in touch.

**Thank you for helping us be there for vulnerable children and families when they need us most.**

### Register your interest
